Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on automation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 980. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 15 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the automation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^980 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 15-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
